AI-assisted digest The shortest route through the most consequential retained party activity. Every point opens its supporting record.

  • FPÖ The Freedom Party of Austria issued multiple parliamentary interventions and public statements criticizing the ÖVP government's agricultural policies, including demands for ministerial resignations over drought relief measures, opposition to EU Mercosur and… Policy proposal
  • Vox Vox leader Santiago Abascal accused Prime Minister Pedro Sánchez of being "the candidate of Morocco" in the context of upcoming elections scheduled for 29 November. Election or campaign
  • NPA-Révolutionnaires The NPA-Révolutionnaires calls for workers and the public to join high school students in a national strike and street demonstrations on Tuesday, October 6, 2026, to protest police repression, social injustice, and the government's 2027 budget cuts. Mobilisation or protest
  • SYRIZA SYRIZA-PS parliamentary group president Rena Doúrou stated the party can guarantee a programmatic agreement to remove the right-wing government and criticized the government's handling of metro construction damage in Kypseli and flood damage in Lasithi. Public statement
  • FdI Minister Francesco Lollobrigida announced new fuel discounts of approximately 20% for the agricultural and fishing sectors through an agreement with Eni, adding to existing government measures including tax credits and excise cuts. Policy proposal

AT2026-10-05#
party document Public statement

Bundeskanzler Stocker muss Chaos-Koalition sofort beenden

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz demands immediate new elections and an end to the ÖVP-SPÖ coalition government, criticizing SPÖ internal chaos and Chancellor Stocker for maintaining the coalition.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Was sich in diesem roten Marxisten-Stadel abspielt, ist ein unwürdiges Schauspiel am Rücken der österreichischen Steuerzahler. Die SPÖ missbraucht unsere Republik als Reha-Klinik für gescheiterte linke Träumer und Sesselkleber.
What is happening in this red Marxist barn is an undignified spectacle at the expense of Austrian taxpayers. The SPÖ is abusing our Republic as a rehab clinic for failed left-wing dreamers and seat-clingers.
Michael Schnedlitz
ÖVP-Kanzler Christian Stocker darf diesem Kasperltheater nicht länger tatenlos zuschauen, nur weil ihm sein eigener Kanzlersessel anscheinend wichtiger ist als das Wohl unserer Heimat.
ÖVP Chancellor Christian Stocker must not continue to watch this puppet theater idly, just because his own chancellor's seat is apparently more important to him than the welfare of our homeland.
Michael Schnedlitz

AT2026-10-05#
party document Public statement

Lagerhaus-Kahlschlag: Fisch beginnt am Kopf zu stinken

FPÖ agricultural spokesperson Peter Schmiedlechner criticized the planned closure of two Raiffeisen Lagerhaus locations and dismissal of over 100 of 460 employees, demanding accountability from management and ÖVP officials and calling for a comprehensive social plan if jobs cannot be saved.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Jetzt müssen nicht nur Management, Vorstand und Aufsichtsrat Rede und Antwort stehen. In diesem Fall kann man nicht einfach so zur Tagesordnung übergehen. Denn: Es geht nicht nur um Zahlen auf dem Papier, sondern um Arbeitsplätze, Existenzen, Familien und um über Jahrzehnte gewachsene regionale Strukturen
Now not only management, board and supervisory board must answer questions. In this case one cannot simply move on to business as usual. Because: It's not just about numbers on paper, but about jobs, livelihoods, families and regional structures that have grown over decades
Peter Schmiedlechner
Regionalität darf kein ÖVP-Wahlkampf-Slogan sein. Wer Regionalität predigt, muss auch für Regionalität kämpfen, wenn es ernst wird
Regionality must not be an ÖVP campaign slogan. Whoever preaches regionality must also fight for regionality when things get serious
Peter Schmiedlechner

party document Policy proposal

News

The Freedom Party of Austria issued multiple parliamentary interventions and public statements criticizing the ÖVP government's agricultural policies, including demands for ministerial resignations over drought relief measures, opposition to EU Mercosur and renaturalization policies, and accusations that the government prioritizes foreign aid over domestic farmers.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
ÖVP muss um Standorte und Jobs kämpfen, statt zur Tagesordnung überzugehen.
The ÖVP must fight for locations and jobs instead of moving on to the next item on the agenda.
Freedom Party of Austria
Preisexplosion bei Pellets entlarvt den Klima-Wahn
Price explosion in pellets exposes the climate madness
Freedom Party of Austria

party document Public statement

Neuwahlen jetzt!

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz demands immediate new elections and the end of the ÖVP-SPÖ-NEOS coalition government, citing SPÖ internal crisis and alleged inability to govern, calling for Herbert Kickl as 'Volkskanzler'.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Sollte Babler gehen, dann muss auch diese gesamte Verlierer-Koalition endgültig zusammenpacken
If Babler goes, then this entire loser coalition must finally pack up
Michael Schnedlitz
In ihrer aktuellen Verfassung ist die SPÖ schlichtweg nicht regierungsfähig
In its current state, the SPÖ is simply not capable of governing
Michael Schnedlitz

SPÖ als Regierungspartei völlig untragbar

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz called for immediate new elections, declaring the SPÖ unfit to govern due to internal crisis and demanding that Herbert Kickl as 'Volkskanzler' replace the current coalition government.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die SPÖ stürzt selbst in die absolute Bedeutungslosigkeit. Diese Truppe, die aktuell nur noch mit sich selbst beschäftigt ist, hat zurzeit schlichtweg keine Kapazitäten, um zu regieren. So ist die SPÖ mittlerweile mehr eine rote Selbsthilfegruppe als eine politische Partei
The SPÖ is plunging itself into absolute irrelevance. This group, which is currently only occupied with itself, simply has no capacity to govern at the moment. The SPÖ has now become more of a red self-help group than a political party
Michael Schnedlitz
Mit einer Selbsthilfegruppe ist zurzeit einfach kein Staat zu machen. Österreich braucht keine Politiker, die aktuell nur um Posten schachern, sondern eine Führung, die für die eigene Bevölkerung arbeitet. Es braucht sofortige Neuwahlen und einen Volkskanzler Herbert Kickl, um den rot-schwarz-pinken Scherbenhaufen endlich wegzuräumen!
You cannot run a state with a self-help group at the moment. Austria does not need politicians who are currently only haggling over positions, but leadership that works for its own population. Immediate new elections are needed and a People's Chancellor Herbert Kickl to finally clear away the red-black-pink pile of rubble!
Michael Schnedlitz

SPÖ-nahe Journalisten-NGO warnt vor ORF-Volksbegehren

FPÖ General Secretary Christian Hafenecker promoted the party's ORF referendum initiative calling for abolition of mandatory broadcasting fees, criticizing SPÖ-linked NGO 'Reporter ohne Grenzen' and its president Fritz Hausjell for opposing the measure.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Wenn ein Mitglied des Bundes sozialdemokratischer Akademiker, das dem ‚Expertenrat' von SPÖ-Chef und Vizekanzler Andreas Babler im letzten Nationalratswahlkampf angehört hat, sich angesichts unseres ORF-Volksbegehrens und der mit diesem erhobenen Forderung nach einer Abschaffung der ORF-Zwangsgebühr um die ‚Unabhängigkeit' des ORF sorgt, geht es ihm nur um den Erhalt des öffentlich-rechtlichen Rundfunks als Propaganda-Lautsprecher der Regierung und des Systems.
When a member of the Federation of Social Democratic Academics, who belonged to the 'expert council' of SPÖ leader and Vice Chancellor Andreas Babler in the last National Council election campaign, worries about the 'independence' of the ORF in light of our ORF referendum and its demand for abolition of the mandatory ORF fee, he is only concerned with preserving public broadcasting as a propaganda loudspeaker for the government and the system.
Christian Hafenecker

Clean information, no luxury salaries and privileges, no mandatory fee, that is the ORF as Austrians want it!
Christian Hafenecker

Doppelte Entlastung: Paketsteuer zurücknehmen – Regierung zurücktreten

FPÖ General Secretary Michael Schnedlitz demanded the withdrawal of a package tax law and called for the resignation of the ÖVP-SPÖ-NEOS coalition government, criticizing new tax burdens on citizens.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Während die teuerste Regierung aller Zeiten das Geld mit beiden Händen aus dem Fenster wirft, schnallt sie den Gürtel bei der Bevölkerung immer enger. Das hat nun ein Maß erreicht, bei dem die rote Linie längst überschritten ist
While the most expensive government of all time throws money out the window with both hands, it tightens the belt ever more on the population. This has now reached a level where the red line has long been crossed
Michael Schnedlitz
Doppelte Entlastung heißt für uns: Paketsteuer zurücknehmen und Regierung zurücktreten. Österreich braucht eine Politik, die ihre Ausgaben überprüft, klare Prioritäten setzt und die Bevölkerung nicht mit immer neuen Abgaben belastet
Double relief means for us: withdraw the package tax and government resign. Austria needs a policy that reviews its spending, sets clear priorities and does not burden the population with ever new levies
Michael Schnedlitz

party document Policy proposal

Genossenschaftswohnungen müssen leistbar bleiben

The FPÖ opposes planned rent increases in cooperative housing affecting 270,000 tenants, criticizing government coalition parties (ÖVP, SPÖ, and especially NEOS) for threatening affordable housing and announcing a parliamentary inquiry directed at Housing Minister Andreas Babler.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Der gemeinnützige Wohnbau muss weiterhin ein Ort der Sicherheit für seine unzähligen Bewohner bleiben. Dramatische Mietensteigerungen – mit denen insbesondere die Neos anscheinend liebäugeln – sind besonders hier kategorisch abzulehnen
Non-profit housing must continue to be a place of security for its countless residents. Dramatic rent increases – which the NEOS in particular apparently are flirting with – must be categorically rejected here
Markus Tschank
Wir Freiheitlichen bekennen uns klar zum System der Wohnungsgemeinnützigkeit. Anscheinend betreiben allerdings ÖVP, SPÖ und besonders die Neos deren faktische Abschaffung: Denn nichts anderes wäre eine Mietenexplosion in 270.000 genossenschaftlichen Mietwohnungen
We Freedom Party members clearly commit ourselves to the system of non-profit housing. However, it appears that the ÖVP, SPÖ and especially the NEOS are pursuing its de facto abolition: Because a rent explosion in 270,000 cooperative rental apartments would be nothing else
Markus Tschank

AT2026-10-02#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Dienstzeit-Desaster bei Polizei bleibt trotz "Einlenken" Karners bestehen

FPÖ deputy security spokesperson Reinhold Maier criticized ÖVP Interior Minister Gerhard Karner's new police duty-time model as a cost-cutting measure that burdens officers and reduces street patrols, demanding the model be immediately stopped and the minister resign.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Was ÖVP-Innenminister Karner hier als großes ‚Einlenken' verkauft, ist nichts anderes als ein kläglicher Versuch, sein gescheitertes Sparpaket PR-mäßig zu retten und die Exekutivbeamten für dumm zu verkaufen. Dieses Täuschungsmanöver ändert nichts an der Tatsache, dass es sich um einen Anschlag auf die Motivation unserer Beamten und die Sicherheit im Land handelt. Karner hat auf ganzer Linie versagt, sollte die Konsequenzen daraus ziehen und noch heute seinen Hut nehmen!
What ÖVP Interior Minister Karner is selling here as a great 'concession' is nothing other than a pathetic attempt to salvage his failed austerity package in PR terms and to take the executive officers for fools. This deception maneuver changes nothing about the fact that this is an attack on the motivation of our officers and the security in the country. Karner has failed on all fronts, should draw the consequences and resign today!
Reinhold Maier
Zentrale ‚Giftzähne' dieser Reform bleiben bestehen. Genau diese Punkte führen aber zu massiven Einkommensverlusten für die Beamten und, was noch viel schlimmer ist, zu einer drastischen Reduzierung der Streifen auf unseren Straßen. Das ist ein direkter Anschlag auf die Sicherheit der österreichischen Bevölkerung, nur um das Budget zu sanieren!
Central 'poison fangs' of this reform remain in place. But precisely these points lead to massive income losses for the officers and, what is much worse, to a drastic reduction of patrols on our streets. This is a direct attack on the security of the Austrian population, just to balance the budget!
Reinhold Maier

party document Public statement

Erhöhung der Krankenkassenprämien: Die Bevölkerung darf nicht die Zeche zahlen!

The Swiss Party of Labour condemns the announced five percent increase in health insurance premiums for 2027 and demands fundamental healthcare policy reform including a public health service, income-based financing, and a national health plan.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Diese erneute Erhöhung der Krankenkassenprämien ist die unvermeidliche Folge eines Systems, das von Grund auf intransparent und unsozial ist und es einer Vielzahl privater Interessen ermöglicht, sich auf Kosten der Versicherten und einer am Gemeinwohl orientierten Gesundheitspolitik die Taschen zu füllen
This renewed increase in health insurance premiums is the inevitable consequence of a system that is fundamentally non-transparent and anti-social and allows a multitude of private interests to line their pockets at the expense of the insured and a health policy oriented toward the common good
Alexander Eniline
Es ist absolut notwendig, die Strategie zu ändern und einen öffentlichen Gesundheitsdienst aufzubauen, der nicht von privaten Akteuren kontrolliert wird und nicht in einem kapitalistischen Wettbewerb steht, dessen oberstes Ziel die Profitmaximierung ist
It is absolutely necessary to change the strategy and build a public health service that is not controlled by private actors and does not stand in capitalist competition whose supreme goal is profit maximization
Timeo Antognini

Stoppt die Repression in der Türkei

The Swiss Party of Labour condemns repression against socialists, LGBTQ+ community, and activists in Turkey following police raids, and expresses solidarity with its sister party SYKP and its co-chair Mertcan Titiz who was arrested during a house search and later released.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz (PdAS) verurteilt das repressive Vorgehen gegen Sozialist:innen, die LGBTQ+-Gemeinschaft und Aktivist:innen in der Türkei.
The Swiss Party of Labour (PdAS) condemns the repressive actions against socialists, the LGBTQ+ community and activists in Turkey.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz
Die PdAS stimmt der SYKP zu, dass willkürliche Inhaftierungen, Unterdrückung und Gewalt gegen Revolutionär:innen, Sozialist:innen und demokratische Oppositionskräfte sofort ein Ende finden müssen.
The PdAS agrees with SYKP that arbitrary detentions, suppression and violence against revolutionaries, socialists and democratic opposition forces must end immediately.
Partei der Arbeit der Schweiz

DE2026-10-02#
party document Public statement

Alice Weidel: Staatsquote muss dringend sinken - Alternative für Deutschland

Alice Weidel criticized the German government's projected state spending ratio of 52 percent for 2026 as an all-time high and called for a leaner state focused on core tasks with lower taxes and spending.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Wenn der Staat über die Hälfte der Wirtschaftsleistung des Landes verschlingt, sollten alle Alarmglocken läuten. Schließlich muss das Geld für die Staatsausgaben ja irgendwo herkommen, weshalb die Steuer- und Abgabenbelastung unter Merz und Klingbeil immer weiter steigt.
When the state devours more than half of the country's economic output, all alarm bells should ring. After all, the money for state spending has to come from somewhere, which is why the tax and levy burden under Merz and Klingbeil keeps rising.
Alice Weidel
Was wir für Deutschland stattdessen brauchen und seitens der Alternative für Deutschland seit langem fordern, ist einen schlanker Staat, der sich auf seine Kernaufgaben konzentriert.
What we need for Germany instead, and what the Alternative for Germany has long demanded, is a leaner state that concentrates on its core tasks.
Alice Weidel

DE2026-10-01#
party document Public statement

Alice Weidel: Steuerverschwendung und Förderirrsinn endlich stoppen - Alternative für Deutschland

Alice Weidel, federal spokesperson for Alternative for Germany, criticized government tax waste and subsidy programs following the publication of the Taxpayers' Association's new Schwarzbuch on October 1, 2026, arguing that state subsidies have distorted market competition and that special debt funds are being misused.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Für all jene, die in Deutschland arbeiten gehen und Jahr für Jahr zig Tausend Euro Steuern zahlen, ist es ein Schlag ins Gesicht, wenn sie das Schwarzbuch des Steuerzahlerbunds lesen.
For all those who go to work in Germany and pay tens of thousands of euros in taxes year after year, it is a slap in the face when they read the Taxpayers' Association's Schwarzbuch.
Alice Weidel
Wir als AfD kritisieren schon lange, dass der Staat jede Menge Projekte fördert, die am Markt nicht überlebensfähig wären.
We as AfD have long criticized that the state subsidizes a lot of projects that would not be viable in the market.
Alice Weidel

DE2026-10-01#
party document Public statement

Stefan Möller: Ministerpräsidenten ändern lieber die Regeln statt den Willen des Volkes zu akzeptieren - Alternative für Deutschland

AfD deputy federal spokesperson Stefan Möller criticized the German Interior Ministers' Conference for changing its decision-making rule from unanimity to a 13-of-16 majority, claiming this change is intended to prevent AfD from blocking decisions if it governs in Saxony-Anhalt.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Das Abrücken vom Einstimmigkeitsprinzip verdeutlicht die immer offener zu Tage tretende Bereitschaft, nach Wahlniederlagen einfach die Regeln zu ändern. Echte Demokraten würden die Niederlage anerkennen. Dieser respektlose Umgang mit dem Votum der Wähler zeigt, von wem die wahre Gefahr für die Demokratie in diesem Land ausgeht.
The departure from the unanimity principle illustrates the increasingly open willingness to simply change the rules after electoral defeats. True democrats would acknowledge the defeat. This disrespectful treatment of the voters' verdict shows where the real danger to democracy in this country comes from.
Stefan Möller

Party documents and direct records

DE2026-10-04#
party document Organisational change

Die HEIMAT Grundsatzprogramm: Einstimmig beschlossen

Die HEIMAT party unanimously adopted a new foundational programme at an extraordinary federal party congress on 3-4 October 2026 in northern Saxony, the first such programme in nearly 16 years, which includes positions on ethnocultural national identity, remigration, EU and NATO withdrawal, and energy policy, alongside a memorial ceremony for former party chairman Udo Voigt.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Das neue Grundsatzprogramm wurde einstimmig im wahrsten Sinne des Wortes angenommen – es gab weder eine einzige Gegenstimme noch eine einzige Enthaltung!
The new foundational programme was adopted unanimously in the truest sense of the word – there was not a single vote against nor a single abstention!
Die HEIMAT
Das deutsche Volk wird klar als gewachsene ethnokulturelle Gemeinschaft aus Herkunft, Sprache, Kultur und Schicksal definiert. Wir bekennen uns zu einem humanistischen Nationalismus: Jedes Volk hat das Recht, es selbst zu bleiben.
The German people are clearly defined as an evolved ethnocultural community of origin, language, culture and destiny. We profess a humanistic nationalism: Every people has the right to remain itself.
Die HEIMAT

Aufbruch im Werra-Meißner-Kreis: Die HEIMAT gewinnt vier Mandate hinzu

Die HEIMAT gained four municipal mandates in Werra-Meißner-Kreis through defections of former AfD members, securing two seats in the district council and two in Wanfried city assembly where they will form their own faction.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Durch Übertritte mehrerer ehemaliger AfD-Mitglieder zur Partei Die HEIMAT im Werra-Meißner-Kreis ist die authentische nationale Oppositionspartei nun mit zwei Abgeordneten im Kreistag sowie mit zwei Mandaten in der Stadtverordnetenversammlung von Wanfried vertreten.
Through defections of several former AfD members to the party Die HEIMAT in Werra-Meißner-Kreis, the authentic national opposition party is now represented with two delegates in the district council as well as with two mandates in the city assembly of Wanfried.
HEIMAT Hessen
Die HEIMAT reicht jedem Patrioten die Hand, der sich konstruktiv für unser Volk einsetzen möchte.
Die HEIMAT extends a hand to every patriot who wants to work constructively for our people.
HEIMAT Hessen

Die Linke chair Ines Schwerdtner criticizes the federal coalition government's austerity course, calling for a social plan instead of further cuts and demanding a genuine policy reversal rather than a compromise between CDU and SPD.

party document Public statement

Übergewinne besteuern statt Tankrabatt von Steuerzahlern bezahlen lassen

Die Linke party chair Ines Schwerdtner criticizes the federal government's renewed fuel discount as taxpayer-funded and ineffective, demanding instead that excess profits of oil companies be taxed to relieve citizens and make the Deutschlandticket cheaper.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Die Bundesregierung hat nichts gelernt: Der Tankrabatt wird direkt aus den Taschen der Steuerzahler bezahlt und löst keines der Probleme in dieser Ölpreiskrise.
The federal government has learned nothing: The fuel discount is paid directly from the pockets of taxpayers and solves none of the problems in this oil price crisis.
Ines Schwerdtner
Stattdessen müssen endlich die Milliarden an Übergewinnen der Mineralölkonzerne dafür genutzt werden, um die Menschen im Land wirklich zu entlasten.
Instead, the billions in excess profits of the oil companies must finally be used to truly relieve the people in the country.
Ines Schwerdtner

FR2026-10-05#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Que fait France Télévision des données de ses spectateurs ?

France Unbowed parliamentary group demands immediate hearing of France Télévision CEO Delphine Ernotte before the National Assembly's cultural affairs committee and requests a CNIL investigation into France Télévision's near-transfer of viewer data to Israeli company Terrific.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Afin de faire toute la lumière sur cette affaire qui pourrait être le précurseur d'une véritable opération d'ingérence, le groupe de la France insoumise demande l'audition immédiate de Delphine Ernotte, PDG de France Télévision et des salaries qui ont eu à traiter de ce sujet, devant la commission des affaires culturelles de l'Assemblée nationale et l'ouverture d'une enquête par la CNIL
In order to shed full light on this affair which could be the precursor to a genuine interference operation, the France Unbowed group demands the immediate hearing of Delphine Ernotte, CEO of France Télévision and the employees who dealt with this subject, before the National Assembly's cultural affairs committee and the opening of an investigation by the CNIL
France Unbowed parliamentary group
Après les ingérences israéliennes contre ses candidats aux élections municipales, la France insoumise agira avec vigilance et détermination contre toutes les tentatives de manipulation du débat démocratique et presse le gouvernement d'agir afin de garantir l'intégrité des élections à venir.
After Israeli interference against its candidates in municipal elections, France Unbowed will act with vigilance and determination against all attempts to manipulate democratic debate and urges the government to act to guarantee the integrity of upcoming elections.
France Unbowed

party document Mobilisation or protest

Dans lâEure, le 3 octobre, mobilisation internationale contre les capitalistes de lâagrochimie

The New Anticapitalist Party – Revolutionaries calls for participation in an international demonstration on October 3 against the Syngenta agrochemical plant in Saint-Pierre-de-la-Garenne (Eure), organized by Confédération paysanne, Soulèvements de la Terre, and other groups, demanding expropriation and reconversion of such facilities.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Il est temps d'exproprier ce type d'usines et de reconvertir leur production en produits sanitaires utiles à la population.
It is time to expropriate this type of factory and reconvert their production into sanitary products useful to the population.
NPA Révolutionnaires
Pour les actionnaires de Syngenta, peu importe la couleur du drapeau, ce qui compte c'est la couleur des profits même au détriment de la nature ou de la santé humaine.
For Syngenta shareholders, the color of the flag doesn't matter, what counts is the color of profits even at the expense of nature or human health.
NPA Révolutionnaires

FR2026-10-02#
party document Public statement

Loi intÃ©graleÂ : et maintenantÂ ?

The New Anticapitalist Party – Revolutionaries criticizes the government's proposed comprehensive law against sexual violence following the Lyhanna case, arguing that the promised budget of 1.5 billion euros is insufficient compared to the 3 billion requested by associations, and that the law focuses excessively on repression rather than prevention.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Cette loi sera peut-être une avancée sur le plan de la reconnaissance des VSS. Mais il faut se souvenir que les violences sexuelles sont des violences sociales, contre lesquelles on ne lutte pas avec les moyens du système qui les engendre.
This law may perhaps be a step forward in terms of recognizing sexual and gender-based violence. But we must remember that sexual violence is social violence, against which we cannot fight with the means of the system that generates it.
Mona Netcha
Les associations chiffrent à 3 milliards le montant de départ qui serait nécessaire pour commencer à mettre en place les mesures décrites dans la loi. Pour le moment, le gouvernement promet une somme plancher de 1,5 milliard dans le budget… L'équivalent de la somme engagé en 2026.
Associations estimate that 3 billion would be the starting amount necessary to begin implementing the measures described in the law. For now, the government promises a floor amount of 1.5 billion in the budget... The equivalent of the amount committed in 2026.
New Anticapitalist Party – Revolutionaries

ΜέΡΑ25 app

MeRA25 demands immediate compensation for citizens, farmers, and municipalities affected by flooding in Crete, criticizing the government for slow response to previous disasters and calling for urgent state support without bureaucratic delays.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το τριήμερο κύμα κακοκαιρίας που έπληξε την Κρήτη ήταν αρκετό για να καταρρεύσει ο μύθος της ανάπτυξης, των σύγχρονων υποδομών και των μεγάλων επενδύσεων στο νησί.
The three-day wave of bad weather that hit Crete was enough to collapse the myth of development, modern infrastructure and large investments on the island.
MeRA25
Η αποκατάσταση δεν είναι κυβερνητική χάρη. Είναι υποχρέωση της πολιτείας απέναντι στους ανθρώπους που δεν μπόρεσε να προστατεύσει, όπως είχε αποστολή.
Restoration is not a government favor. It is the state's obligation to the people it failed to protect, as was its mission.

GR2026-10-03#
party document Public statement

ΜέΡΑ25 app

Yanis Varoufakis issued a statement on the anniversary of German reunification criticizing Germany's militarization, increased arms exports to Israel (which rose from 32 million to 326 million euros since October 2023), police repression of pro-Palestinian protesters including German Jews, and the economic stagnation that he argues has fueled the rise of the AfD party.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Το γερμανικό κράτος μετατρέπεται σε οπλοστάσιο της Ευρώπης. Πρώην εργοστάσια της Volkswagen παράγουν πλέον άρματα μάχης Leopard — ο Μιλιταριστικός Κεϋνσιανισμός έχει καταστεί η μοναδική στρατηγική ανάπτυξης της Γερμανίας.
The German state is being transformed into the arsenal of Europe. Former Volkswagen factories now produce Leopard battle tanks — Militaristic Keynesianism has become Germany's sole development strategy.
Yanis Varoufakis
Γερμανικά όπλα ρέουν στο Ισραήλ — η Γερμανία είναι ο δεύτερος μεγαλύτερος προμηθευτής όπλων του Ισραήλ, μετά τις Ηνωμένες Πολιτείες, με τις εξαγωγές όπλων να έχουν δεκαπλασιαστεί από τον Οκτώβριο του 2023, από 32 εκατομμύρια ευρώ σε 326 εκατομμύρια ευρώ.
German weapons flow to Israel — Germany is the second largest arms supplier to Israel, after the United States, with arms exports having increased tenfold since October 2023, from 32 million euros to 326 million euros.
Yanis Varoufakis

MeRA25 issued a statement condemning the Greek government's use of taxpayer and EU funds for a video campaign promoting what the party calls racist migration policies, demanding safe refugee access to Europe, fast asylum processing, equal rights, and opposing refugee transfers to poor countries.

Η κυβέρνηση Μητσοτάκη πετά πλέον κάθε πρόσχημα ανθρωπισμού σε σχέση με το μεταναστευτικό.
The Mitsotakis government now throws away every pretense of humanism in relation to migration.
MeRA25
Με χρήση κρατικών, αλλά και ευρωπαϊκών πόρων, οι πολίτες γίνονται κάθε μέρα αποδέκτες μίας προπαγάνδας που κανονικοποιεί και εξευγενίζει τις πολιτικές της «Ευρώπης Φρούριο» και τις μαζικές απελάσεις ανθρώπων που επιχειρούν να ξεφύγουν από τον πόλεμο και την ανέχεια.

GR2026-10-03#
party document Public statement

Δ. Νατσιός στο Action 24: Η λέξη “υποχώρηση” είναι πολύ βαριά για τα εθνικά θέματα -Τον λαό τον κυβερνάς με ταπείνωση και όχι ταπεινώνοντάς τον

Dimitris Natsios, president of NIKH, gave a television interview criticizing government policy on fuel taxation and refineries' profits, condemning what he called government retreat on the Kasos incident with Turkey, rejecting potential alliances with Samaras, Latinopoulou, and Karystianou, and attacking Alexis Tsipras's visit to Mount Athos and his new party name.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Έχω συγκριτικούς πίνακες από τα δύο μεγάλα διυλιστήρια της χώρας. Το πρώτο εξάμηνο του 2025 είχαν κέρδη 291 εκατομμύρια, ενώ το πρώτο εξάμηνο του 2026 έφτασαν τα 1,082 δισεκατομμύρια -αύξηση 300%. Όταν έχουν τέτοια τρομακτική αύξηση στα καθαρά κέρδη, δεν είναι αισχροκέρδεια αυτό; Δεν μπορεί η κυβέρνηση να επιβάλει δικαιοσύνη;
I have comparative tables from the two major refineries of the country. In the first half of 2025 they had profits of 291 million, while in the first half of 2026 they reached 1.082 billion - an increase of 300%. When they have such a tremendous increase in net profits, isn't this profiteering? Can't the government impose justice?
Dimitris Natsios
Η λέξη 'υποχώρηση' είναι πάρα πολύ βαριά για τα εθνικά θέματα. Όταν μιλάμε εμείς για κατευνασμό μέσα στη Βουλή, αυτό ακριβώς εννοούμε. Στην Κάσο το Πολεμικό Ναυτικό αποχώρησε. Αν ήμουν εγώ, θα έλεγα 'δεν φεύγετε'. Και πυρ θα άνοιγα αν οι Τούρκοι παραβίαζαν τα χωρικά μας ύδατα.
The word 'retreat' is very heavy for national issues. When we speak of appeasement in Parliament, this is exactly what we mean. At Kasos the Navy withdrew. If it were me, I would say 'you are not leaving'. And I would open fire if the Turks violated our territorial waters.
Dimitris Natsios

GR2026-10-05#
party document Parliamentary intervention

Να ληφθούν έκτακτα μέτρα ανακούφισης και να αξιοποιηθούν οι παρεκκλίσεις για τον αφθώδη πυρετό στη Λέσβο

SYRIZA MEP Kostas Arvanitis submitted a written question to the European Commission requesting emergency relief measures and utilization of regulatory derogations to protect livestock farming on Lesvos following a prolonged foot-and-mouth disease outbreak.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Την ανάγκη αξιοποίησης όλων των διαθέσιμων δυνατοτήτων που προβλέπει η ευρωπαϊκή νομοθεσία για την προστασία της κτηνοτροφίας στη Λέσβο, μετά την παρατεταμένη επιζωοτία αφθώδους πυρετού, αναδεικνύει με νέα ερώτησή του προς την Ευρωπαϊκή Επιτροπή ο ευρωβουλευτής του ΣΥΡΙΖΑ και Αντιπρόεδρος της LEFT, Κώστας Αρβανίτης.
The need to utilize all available possibilities provided by European legislation for the protection of livestock farming on Lesvos, following the prolonged foot-and-mouth disease outbreak, is highlighted by SYRIZA MEP and Vice-President of the LEFT, Kostas Arvanitis, with a new question to the European Commission.
ΣΥΡΙΖΑ
Σε μια κατάσταση που απειλεί όχι μόνο το ζωικό κεφάλαιο αλλά και την ίδια την παραγωγική συνέχεια του νησιού, είναι κρίσιμο να αξιοποιηθούν πλήρως οι δυνατότητες που προβλέπει το ευρωπαϊκό κανονιστικό πλαίσιο.
In a situation that threatens not only the livestock but also the productive continuity of the island itself, it is critical to fully utilize the possibilities provided by the European regulatory framework.
Κώστας Αρβανίτης

GR2026-10-04#
party document Public statement

«Η Αριστερά δεν χρειάζεται άλλον ένα Μεσσία…»

SYRIZA Organisational Secretary Giannis Boulekos outlined the party's economic programme including a 13th pension and salary, VAT and fuel tax cuts, public control of strategic sectors, and housing protection, while rejecting personalist politics and calling for a programmatic left-progressive alliance.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Η Αριστερά δεν χρειάζεται άλλον έναν Μεσσία. Χρειάζεται πρόγραμμα, κοινωνικές ρίζες και συλλογικότητα.
The Left does not need another Messiah. It needs a programme, social roots and collectivity.
Giannis Boulekos
Ο ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ καταθέτει διαφορετικές προτεραιότητες: 13η σύνταξη και 13ο μισθό, ουσιαστική μείωση της έμμεσης φορολογίας, παρέμβαση στο κόστος των καυσίμων, δημόσιο έλεγχο σε στρατηγικούς τομείς της οικονομίας, προστασία της πρώτης κατοικίας, αντιμετώπιση του ιδιωτικού χρέους και ένα μεγάλο πρόγραμμα κοινωνικής κατοικίας.
SYRIZA-PS puts forward different priorities: 13th pension and 13th salary, substantial reduction of indirect taxation, intervention in fuel costs, public control in strategic sectors of the economy, protection of primary residence, tackling private debt and a major social housing programme.
Giannis Boulekos

GR2026-10-03#
party document Mobilisation or protest

Ρένα Δούρου: Οι κάτοικοι της Κυψέλης ζητούν να είναι ασφαλείς στα σπίτια τους – Η κυβέρνηση κρύβεται

SYRIZA-PS delegation led by parliamentary group president Rena Dourou participated in a protest by Kypseli residents over building damage from Metro construction works, demanding relocation, compensation, and government accountability.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Πριν λίγες μέρες μπήκαμε στα σπίτια αυτών των ανθρώπων, είδαμε τις ρωγμές, είδαμε την αγωνία τους. Σήμερα είμαστε ξανά εδώ, μαζί τους. Οι κάτοικοι της Κυψέλης δεν ζητούν χάρη. Ζητούν να είναι ασφαλείς μέσα στα ίδια τους τα σπίτια και απέναντι τους βρίσκουν μια κυβέρνηση που κρύβεται πίσω από καθυστερήσεις και μισές αλήθειες.
A few days ago we entered these people's homes, we saw the cracks, we saw their anguish. Today we are here again, with them. The residents of Kypseli are not asking for a favor. They are asking to be safe inside their own homes and facing them they find a government that hides behind delays and half-truths.
Rena Dourou
Τέμπη, υποκλοπές, ΟΠΕΚΕΠΕ, το ίδιο μοτίβο, όπου υπάρχει ευθύνη, επιχειρείται συγκάλυψη.
Tempi, wiretapping, OPEKEPE, the same pattern, where there is responsibility, a cover-up is attempted.
Rena Dourou

GR2026-10-03#
party document Public statement

Παύλος Πολάκης στην Εφ.Συν.: Πώς η κυβέρνηση Μητσοτάκη πριμοδοτούσε τα ιδιωτικά ιατρεία… ευζωίας

SYRIZA health spokesperson Pavlos Polakis accused the Mitsotakis government of promoting private wellness clinics through legislative changes from 2022-2023 and alleged financial irregularities involving Athens Medical Association president Georgios Patoulis and private healthcare companies.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Τέτοιο πράγμα πριν δεν υπήρχε. Και το λέω μετά πλήρους λόγου γνώσεως. Τέτοια ξεφτίλα, τέτοια διάχυση αυτής της παραϊατρικής πριν το 2019 δεν υπήρχε.
Such a thing did not exist before. And I say this with full knowledge. Such disgrace, such diffusion of this para-medical practice did not exist before 2019.
Pavlos Polakis
Πηγαίνει ο υπουργός Υγείας και διαφημίζει μια ιδιωτική επένδυση, τη Νότια Medcare στη Γλυφάδα, ως κλινική ευζωίας και μακροζωίας. Τεράστια επένδυση. Από τα Ηνωμένα Αραβικά Εμιράτα.
The Minister of Health goes and advertises a private investment, Notia Medcare in Glyfada, as a wellness and longevity clinic. Huge investment. From the United Arab Emirates.
Pavlos Polakis

Στα Τέμπη έγινε έγκλημα και στο Πολυτεχνείο η χούντα σκότωσε

SYRIZA-PS condemned journalist Stamatis Zacharos for denying the existence of Errieta Molcho among Tempi train crash victims and for repeating junta-era claims that no one died at the Polytechnic uprising, pledging to continue fighting for justice and against far-right hate rhetoric.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Ο ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ δεν κρύβει και δεν ωραιοποιεί την αλήθεια. Μάχεται για την ανάδειξή της.
SYRIZA-PS does not hide or beautify the truth. It fights for its revelation.
ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ
Καταδικάζουμε αυτές τις αναφορές και δηλώνουμε ξεκάθαρα ότι θα συνεχίσουμε τον αγώνα για Δικαιοσύνη και εκδίωξη της Ακροδεξιάς ρητορικής μίσους από τον δημόσιο χώρο.
We condemn these references and declare clearly that we will continue the struggle for Justice and the expulsion of far-right hate rhetoric from the public space.
ΣΥΡΙΖΑ-ΠΣ

SYRIZA-PS Secretary Nikos Pappas presented the party's 5 billion euro policy package at the Olympia Forum, including proposals for a 13th pension, using EFKA as a national investor for private debt and social housing, and criticized the government's fiscal ceiling claims.

Alternative Budget 2027

People Before Profit launched their Alternative Budget 2027 on 2 October 2026, proposing to tax multi-millionaires to create an affordable Ireland and criticizing the Fianna Fáil and Fine Gael government's Budget 2026 for removing cost-of-living supports and increasing student fees.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated

People Before Profit

IE2026-10-02#
party document Policy proposal

People Before Profit launch Alternative Budget 2027 - "For an Affordable Ireland - Tax the Multi-Millionaires"

People Before Profit launched their Alternative Budget 2027 at Buswells Hotel proposing to raise €45 billion through wealth taxes on multi-millionaires, higher corporation tax, and a financial transactions tax to fund public sector pay increases, housing, free childcare and education, and climate measures.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
Today we launch our alternative Budget 2027 proposals 'For an Affordable Ireland - Tax the Multi-millionaires'.
Richard Boyd Barrett
We now have 11 billionaires in Ireland who have more wealth than 3,500,000 people combined.
Richard Boyd Barrett

Think Left Conference This Weekend in Derry

People Before Profit is hosting its annual Think Left Conference from October 2-5, 2026 in St Columb's Hall, Derry, featuring international speakers, discussions on workers' campaigns, Palestine solidarity, political unionism, Stormont's future, and socialist feminist perspectives on Irish unity.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
This year's Think Left Conference comes at a crucial moment. The conference will deal directly with the big questions we face locally and internationally.
Becca Bor and Darryl Horan
Socialist MLA Gerry Carroll will discuss Stormont's future in the midst of resolute opposition from Garvaghy Road residents to a sectarian Orange Order march, racist pogroms, cuts to public services, MLA pay scandals and budget crisis.
People Before Profit

IE2026-09-29#
party document Legal action

Councillor Takes High Court Legal Challenge as Council Executive Refuse to Implement 15-to-1 Vote to Halt Rent Increases

People Before Profit Councillor Jess Spear has launched a High Court judicial review against South Dublin County Council after its chief executive refused to implement a 15-to-1 council vote to halt rent increases of up to 53% for social housing tenants.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryNot human-validated
The law is very clear on this. The Chief Executive has to follow council policy, and he has to abide by Section 140 motions. Civil servants, no matter how senior, are not a law unto themselves. Ploughing ahead with a rent hike against the express wishes of the elected members looks like a clear and reckless violation of the Local Government Act.
Jess Spear
What is happening in South Dublin should alarm anyone who cares about local democracy. Councillors voted not once but twice on this, and an unelected official has decided to override them. If a Chief Executive can simply ignore a Section 140 motion, then what local democracy do we have left at all?
Paul Murphy

Carburante: nuovi sconti per settore agricolo e pesca

Minister Francesco Lollobrigida announced new fuel discounts of approximately 20% for the agricultural and fishing sectors through an agreement with Eni, adding to existing government measures including tax credits and excise cuts.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated

I want to thank Eni for having fully understood the need to support our primary productive sector, as much as possible, with a fuel access rate that allows them to continue performing the precious task of guardians of the environment and Italian food security.
Francesco Lollobrigida
La riduzione dei prezzi, garantita grazie alla scelta dell'azienda, permette alle imprese agricole e della pesca di ottenere uno sconto di circa il 20% sulle tariffe attuali.
The price reduction, guaranteed thanks to the company's choice, allows agricultural and fishing enterprises to obtain a discount of approximately 20% on current rates.
Francesco Lollobrigida

party document Public statement

Migranti: sì ad hub in Paesi terzi, Ue adotta linea italiana

Brothers of Italy representatives claim vindication as the EU Council approves new return regulations including hubs in third countries, stating this validates Italy's Albania centers approach and proves the left wrong about their legality.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con il via libera definitivo del Consiglio dell'Ue alle nuove norme per i rimpatri gli Stati avranno a disposizione strumenti più efficaci per rendere effettivo il rientro degli immigrati irregolari che non hanno diritto a restare nell'Ue, in particolar modo quelli che rappresentano un rischio per la sicurezza.
With the final approval by the EU Council of the new rules for returns, states will have more effective tools to make the return of irregular immigrants who have no right to remain in the EU effective, especially those who represent a security risk.
Sara Kelany
La sinistra che sosteneva che i centri in Albania fossero contrari al diritto Ue, quindi, ha preso l'ennesima cantonata: erano talmente 'illegittimi' che ora è la stessa Ue che propone di realizzarli.
The left that claimed the centers in Albania were contrary to EU law has therefore made yet another blunder: they were so 'illegitimate' that now the EU itself proposes to create them.
Sara Kelany

party document Public statement

Sanità: 800 nuove prestazioni, governo aggiorna livelli essenziali

Brothers of Italy senators praised the Meloni government's publication of updated Essential Levels of Assistance (LEA) adding approximately 800 new healthcare services to Italy's National Health Service, crediting Health Minister Schillaci for completing a long-awaited reform covering prevention, oncology, rare diseases, and chronic conditions.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con la pubblicazione in Gazzetta Ufficiale dei nuovi Lea, il Servizio sanitario nazionale compie un decisivo passo avanti. L'inserimento di circa 800 nuove prestazioni, servizi e terapie, insieme al superamento di quelle ormai obsolete, consente di adeguare l'offerta sanitaria ai progressi della medicina e alle nuove esigenze dei cittadini.
With the publication in the Official Gazette of the new LEAs, the National Health Service takes a decisive step forward. The inclusion of approximately 800 new services, treatments and therapies, together with the elimination of those now obsolete, allows the healthcare offering to be adapted to medical progress and the new needs of citizens.
Giovanni Satta
Dopo anni di sostanziale immobilismo, il governo Meloni, grazie anche al prezioso lavoro del ministro Schillaci, porta a compimento un aggiornamento atteso da tempo.
After years of substantial immobility, the Meloni government, thanks also to the valuable work of Minister Schillaci, completes a long-awaited update.
Giovanni Satta

IT2026-09-30#
party document Policy proposal

Rai: riforma rende servizio pubblico moderno e plurale

Brothers of Italy members of the RAI oversight commission welcomed committee approval of RAI reform legislation that restructures governance to give Parliament 4 of 7 board seats, employees 1 seat, and the Ministry of Economy only 2 seats, claiming this reverses the 2015 Renzi law's executive control and aligns with European Media Freedom Act requirements.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Accogliamo con soddisfazione l'approvazione in Commissione della riforma della Rai. Si tratta di un passaggio storico che finalmente sana un vulnus aperto oltre dieci anni fa con la legge Renzi e che il centrosinistra quando era al governo non ha mai voluto riformare.
We welcome with satisfaction the committee approval of the RAI reform. This is a historic step that finally heals a wound opened over ten years ago with the Renzi law and that the center-left when in government never wanted to reform.
Fratelli d'Italia
Con le nuove norme, non sarà più l'esecutivo a decidere l'Amministratore Delegato in solitaria, bensì il Consiglio di amministrazione nella sua piena ed esclusiva autonomia statutaria.
With the new rules, it will no longer be the executive deciding the CEO alone, but rather the Board of Directors in its full and exclusive statutory autonomy.
Fratelli d'Italia

Pubblica amministrazione: decreto rafforza efficienza e qualità dei servizi

Brothers of Italy senators announce final approval of a public administration decree that extends municipal reporting deadlines, creates a 187.5 million euro fund for disability and poverty support, reserves up to 20% of public sector positions for flexible contract workers, restores vacation pay indemnities, and authorizes 500 additional State Police hires.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con l'approvazione definitiva del decreto Pa il Governo Meloni e la maggioranza mantengono un impegno preso con i territori.
With the final approval of the PA decree, the Meloni Government and the majority keep a commitment made to the territories.
Paola Mancini
Il nuovo fondo da 187,5 milioni presso il Ministero del Lavoro sosterrà almeno 5.000 persone con disabilità e 25.000 in grave deprivazione materiale.
The new fund of 187.5 million at the Ministry of Labor will support at least 5,000 people with disabilities and 25,000 in severe material deprivation.
Paola Mancini

IT2026-09-30#
party document Policy proposal

Università: riforma consiglio nazionale migliora rappresentanza componenti

Brothers of Italy Senator Ella Bucalo announced the approval of a reform to the National University Council (CUN) that updates its composition and representation, including a Brothers of Italy amendment specifying election procedures for academic representatives and increasing student representation from 13.7% to 18.4%.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Con l'approvazione della riforma del Consiglio Universitario Nazionale si aggiorna un organismo centrale per il sistema universitario italiano, adeguandone composizione, rappresentanza e funzioni all'evoluzione dell'università degli ultimi anni.
With the approval of the reform of the National University Council, a central body for the Italian university system is updated, adapting its composition, representation and functions to the evolution of universities in recent years.
Ella Bucalo
In questo quadro si inserisce l'emendamento di Fratelli d'Italia a mia firma, approvato, con il quale abbiamo precisato che, per ciascuna delle 14 aree disciplinari, viene eletto un professore ordinario e un secondo rappresentante scelto tra professori associati e ricercatori universitari, compresi quelli a tempo determinato.
In this framework, the Brothers of Italy amendment signed by me, which was approved, specifies that for each of the 14 disciplinary areas, a full professor is elected and a second representative chosen from associate professors and university researchers, including those on fixed-term contracts.
Ella Bucalo

IT2026-09-30#
party document Policy proposal

Droga: stretta su furti fentanyl e allerta per cannabis potenziata

Undersecretary to the Prime Minister Alfredo Mantovano announced a new Health Ministry circular published in the Official Gazette establishing security rules for fentanyl custody in healthcare facilities after 39 thefts occurred from the start of 2025 to August, and warned about increased THC potency in cannabis rising from 1.5-2% to an average of 30%.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Per questo è stata pubblicata sulla Gazzetta ufficiale una circolare del Ministero della Salute che supera la frammentazione della gestione del fenomeno e muta la prospettiva: fissa una serie di regole per la sicurezza, la custodia e la tracciabilità
For this reason a circular from the Ministry of Health has been published in the Official Gazette that overcomes the fragmentation of managing the phenomenon and changes the perspective: it establishes a series of rules for security, custody and traceability
Alfredo Mantovano
Continua a essere sottovalutata la pericolosità delle droghe cosiddette leggere, senza comprendere che in passato il Thc, il principio attivo della cannabis, era presente a una percentuale media dell'1,5-2 per cento. Oggi è arrivato alla media del 30
The danger of so-called light drugs continues to be underestimated, without understanding that in the past THC, the active ingredient of cannabis, was present at an average percentage of 1.5-2 percent. Today it has reached an average of 30
Alfredo Mantovano

Covid: con Arcuri clamorose falle e sperpero denaro pubblico

Brothers of Italy deputy Francesco Filini criticized former Covid commissioner Domenico Arcuri's management during a Covid commission hearing, highlighting successor General Figliuolo's testimony about discontinuing large contracts, intermediaries, and advance payments that characterized Arcuri's tenure, including 1.25 billion euros spent on 800 million masks.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Le più clamorose falle della gestione della pandemia di Covid da parte della struttura commissariale di Domenico Arcuri sono emerse oggi, in tutta la loro gravità, durante l'audizione in commissione Covid del generale Francesco Paolo Figliuolo, successore di Arcuri alla guida della struttura commissariale.
The most glaring failures in the management of the Covid pandemic by Domenico Arcuri's commissarial structure emerged today, in all their gravity, during the Covid commission hearing of General Francesco Paolo Figliuolo, Arcuri's successor as head of the commissarial structure.
Francesco Filini
Perché Arcuri non seguì questa elementare prassi considerando che maneggiava rilevanti cifre di soldi pubblici?
Why did Arcuri not follow this elementary practice considering that he was handling significant amounts of public money?
Francesco Filini

party document Public statement

Partido Comunista Português

The Portuguese Communist Party issued a statement supporting Lula da Silva's candidacy in Brazil's presidential runoff election scheduled for October 25, 2026, while condemning US Trump Administration interference in the Brazilian electoral process.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
O que está em jogo nas eleições brasileiras é a defesa da democracia e da soberania do Brasil, do prosseguimento e aprofundamento de um caminho em prol de liberdade, de direitos, de desenvolvimento, que Lula da Silva e as forças democráticas e progressistas brasileiras protagonizam, ou a imposição de um projecto reaccionário, golpista, fascizante, subserviente ao imperialismo norte-americano e apoiado por este, que é protagonizado por Flávio Bolsonaro e os interesses profundamente antidemocráticos e antipatrióticos que este representa.
What is at stake in the Brazilian elections is the defense of democracy and Brazil's sovereignty, the continuation and deepening of a path in favor of freedom, rights, development, which Lula da Silva and the Brazilian democratic and progressive forces champion, or the imposition of a reactionary, coup-plotting, fascist project, subservient to and supported by North American imperialism, which is championed by Flávio Bolsonaro and the profoundly antidemocratic and antipatriotic interests he represents.
Portuguese Communist Party
O PCP denuncia e condena as abertas e inaceitáveis pressões e ingerência da Administração Trump dos EUA, que procura criar as condições para submeter o Brasil aos seus interesses, dominar os seus imensos recursos, colocá-lo ao serviço dos seus intentos de dominação neocolonial da América Latina e das Caraíbas, que ameaçam e agridem a paz, a soberania e os direitos dos povos desta região.
The PCP denounces and condemns the open and unacceptable pressures and interference of the US Trump Administration, which seeks to create conditions to submit Brazil to its interests, dominate its immense resources, place it at the service of its intentions of neocolonial domination of Latin America and the Caribbean, which threaten and attack the peace, sovereignty and rights of the peoples of this region.
Portuguese Communist Party

De tar plats i riksdagens utskott för Vänsterpartiet

The Left Party announced committee assignments for its parliamentary members for the upcoming term, with Nadja Awad as chair of the social insurance committee, Hawar Asaiesh as chair of the education committee, and Anfal Mahdi as vice chair of the civil committee.

Samuel Gonzalez Westling

In my role as responsible for social insurance issues, I will focus on ensuring that no person should have to become poor because they become sick or unemployed, that is what Sweden promises its citizens and we must keep that. party document Parliamentary intervention

Vänsterpartiet kräver omedelbart stopp för tonårsutvisningarna

The Left Party submitted a parliamentary committee initiative on 30 September 2026 to immediately stop deportations of teenagers who grew up in Sweden but are expelled when they turn 18, proposing changes to immigration law including raising the core family age to 21 and reinstating special compassionate grounds for residence permits.

Source metadataAI-assisted screening and summaryAI translationNot human-validated
Vi gick till val på att stoppa tonårsutvisningarna och nu ska vi göra det en gång för alla
We went to the election on stopping the teenage deportations and now we will do it once and for all
Nooshi Dadgostar
Det måste gå snabbt. Vi har inte tid att vänta in den allmänna motionstiden eller en ny regering. Framför allt har inte dessa ungdomar den här tiden. Förra mandatperioden stoppades våra förslag eftersom vi inte hade majoritet. Nu har vi det
It must go quickly. We don't have time to wait for the general motion period or a new government. Above all, these young people don't have this time. Last term our proposals were stopped because we didn't have a majority. Now we do
Samuel Gonzalez Westling
